We need it, and it is right that we have a sense of urgency. That sense is an expression of the creative force in us. Without a sense of urgency,
we do not awaken to a new level of consciousness and do not move beyond materialism.
I am often asked whether as a businessperson I feel conflict in championing a shift beyond materialism. My answer is that I think not. We are challenged as businesspersons to resolve our internal conflicts and to pursue the opportunity to do business with these conflicts resolved. Without challenges, we do not evolve. As a wise man once said, a life unexamined is a life not lived. That is why I always say, “Live a purposeful life, and live it mindfully.”
(II) The Tao Experience of The Source
Zhi-Gang Sha KONG, KONG, KONG
I have overcome many huge blockages in my spiritual journey, health, relationships, and many other aspects. I have had many Aha moments and realizations regarding the deepest spiritual wisdom. The insight I gained can be summarized in one word: kong (空). Kong means “emptiness.”
In Chinese culture, there are three belief systems: Buddhism, Taoism, and Confucianism. They have one thing in common: their highest teaching and achievement is described as emptiness. In Buddhist teaching, the renowned statement is si da jie kong (四大皆空). Si da means four big things including earth, fire, water, and wind. Jie means “all.” Kong means “emptiness.” Si da jie kong means earth, fire, water, and wind are impermanent and dependent on other things for their continued existence—meaning, essentially, emptiness. To reach kong is to reach the Buddha’s state or condition.
In Taoist teaching the renowned phrase is chang qing jing (常清静). Chang means “permanent.” Qing jing means “complete quietness and peace,” which is the state of emptiness. Chang qing jing means to always be in the state of emptiness.
In Confucius’s teaching, there is also a renowned phrase: wang wo ru kong (忘我入空). Wang means “forget.” Wo means “myself.” Ru means “enter.” Kong means “emptiness.” Wang wo ru kong means to forget yourself and enter the emptiness condition.
Millions of Buddhist practitioners wish to become a Buddha. Buddha means the highest enlightened being with the highest wisdom and abilities. Hence, to become a Buddha is the highest goal for all Buddhists pursuing
their spiritual journey. Similarly, millions of Taoist practitioners wish to reach Tao. To reach Tao
means to reach longevity and become immortal, which is the highest goal of all Taoists in their spiritual journey.
In the same way, millions of Confucian practitioners wish to become saints. To reach the condition of wang wo ru kong is the highest achievement of the Confucian saints.
So then, what is the key to reach Buddha, to reach Tao, and to reach wang wo ru kong? These dreams have been pursued by millions of people from ancient times to the present. My understanding can be summarized in one word: kong, or emptiness.
In ancient wisdom, The Source created Heaven and Mother Earth. Heaven is yang. Mother Earth is yin. Source, Heaven, and Mother Earth further created countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes, including human beings. To become a Buddha, to reach Tao, and to become a saint is to return to The Source, which is to meld one’s shen qi jing with Source shen qi jing. Shen includes soul, heart, and mind. Qi means “energy.” Jing means “matter.”
Soul is spirit. In quantum science, soul is information. Soul, spirit, and information are the same. Heart is the core of life. Mind includes the superficial consciousness, deep consciousness, subconsciousness, and the superconscious.
Albert Einstein’s equation of relativity is E = mc2. Relativity is the relationship between energy and matter. Tao science, which Dr. Rulin Xiu and I have created based on quantum science, shared with humanity that the equation of grand unification is S + E + M = 1. S means shen, including soul, heart, and mind, E means energy, M means matter, and 1 means Source field.
How can a spiritual being become a Buddha, reach Tao, and become a saint? The key is to enter kong. Kong is Buddha, Tao, and the highest state of sainthood. To reach kong is to transform one’s polluted shen qi jing to the shen qi jing of The Source.
To transform polluted shen qi jing includes transforming shen blockages. Shen blockages include soul, heart, and mind blockages. Soul blockages are negative karma. Negative karma means a person and their ancestors have harmed others in their lives.
Heart blockages include tan, chen, and chi (贪, 嗔, 痴). Tan means “greed.” Chen means “anger.” Chi means “lack of wisdom.” Heart blockages also
include selfishness, impurities, and more. Mind blockages include negative mind-sets, negative beliefs, negative
attitudes, ego, and attachments. Qi or energy blockages are between the cells and organs. Jing or matter blockages are inside the cells. Shen qi jing blockages must be completely removed in order to become a
Buddha, to reach Tao, or to become a saint. Why are millions of people on the spiritual journey? Only a few have
become a Buddha, reached Tao, or reached the highest condition of being a saint. To remove all shen qi jing blockages is only half of the condition to reach kong, which is to become a Buddha, to reach Tao, or to become a saint.
What is the other half of the condition to reach kong? In my understanding, it is to possess the Ten Da qualities. Ten Da are the nature of Source, heaven, Buddha, Tao, and the highest saints.
The Ten Da qualities include:
Da ai (大愛), greatest love Da kuan shu (大寬恕), greatest forgiveness Da ci bei (大慈悲), greatest compassion Da guang ming (大光明), greatest light Da qian bei (大謙卑), greatest humility Da he xie (大和諧), greatest harmony Da chang sheng (大昌盛), greatest flourishing Da gan en (大感恩), greatest gratitude Da fu wu (大服務), greatest service Da yuan man (大圓滿), greatest enlightenment
Da ai, greatest love, unconditional love, can melt all blockages, transform all life, and enlighten our soul, heart, mind, and body. Everyone and everything in countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes needs greatest love. How do we achieve greatest love? Think about the Sun. The Sun shines on all things. Does the Sun think that you are kind and compassionate and therefore shines on you? If you are selfish or mean, will the Sun not shine on you? The Sun has the da ai nature. The Sun shines on everyone and everything, which is unconditional love. Da ai is the first quality to reach kong, which is to become a Buddha, to reach Tao, or to become a saint.
“Da Kuan Shu,” greatest forgiveness. Greatest forgiveness is for a family, for an organization, for all spiritual realms, for all nations, for all living things
and non-living things. The essence of “Da Kuan Shu,” greatest forgiveness is, “I forgive you. You forgive me. Bring love, peace, and harmony.” This is the second nature of Source to reach kong.
“Da Ci Bei,” greatest compassion. Greatest compassion increases willpower. Willpower is a major key for success in every aspect of life, including health, relationships, and finances. Serve humanity. Serve others. You will gain immeasurable virtue. Virtue is the record of one’s service. This is the quality of “Da Ci Bei,” greatest compassion. This is the third Source nature to reach kong.
“Da Guang Ming,” greatest light. Source light is the highest light. Source light is invisible to the normal human being’s eyes. It could be visible in one’s advanced spiritual eyes. Whether one can see Source light or not, Source light is in countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes. Source light is in every aspect of life. Source light is in every system, every organ, every cell, every DNA and RNA. The issue is that the shen qi jing blockages of a human being block Source light from shining enough in the body from head to toe, skin to bone, as well as in a family, an organization, or a country.
What is one’s spiritual journey? One key is to allow Source light to shine within and outside the body. To shine Source light in every aspect of your life, allow Source light to shine in your country, in Mother Earth, and in countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes. How can you allow Source light to shine more? Do a spiritual practice. Chant Source light. Visualize Source light. Absorb Source light. Meld with Source light. “Da Guang Ming” is the fourth Source nature to reach kong.
“Da Qian Bei,” greatest humility. In ancient spiritual teaching, there is a renowned phrase, humility will benefit every aspect of life; ego will harm every aspect of life. There is an ancient statement, “Rou Ruo Sheng Gang Qiang 柔弱勝鋼強.” “Rou” means gentle. “Ruo” means soft. “Sheng” means overcome. “Gang Qiang” means hard and strong. “Rou Ruo Sheng Gang Qiang” means gentle and soft overcome the hardest and strongest.
Think about water that continually drips, drop by drop, on a rock for a long, long time. It will make a hole in the rock. This is the soft overcoming the hardest. Another example is to think about a human being’s teeth and tongue when they get older. The teeth are much harder than the tongue. Do the teeth live longer or does the tongue live longer? For “Da Qian Bei,” greatest humility, the key quality is gentle and soft. There is no competition and fighting. This is one of the highest natures of an enlightened being.
Greatest humility also appears as persistently moving forward with courage. If one is humble, every aspect of life progresses further.
The opposite aspect of humility is ego. There is another renowned statement from a spiritual father in China named Wang Yang Ming, “Ego is the biggest enemy for one’s life.” The moment one has ego, every aspect of life starts to decrease, whether one realizes it or not. To have humility, to prevent ego, is one key to go into kong. “Da Qian Bei” is the fifth Source quality to reach kong.
“Da He Xie,” greatest harmony. In the I Ching, an ancient sacred teaching, there is a sacred phrase, “San Ren Tong Xin, Qi Li Duan Jin 三人同
心, 齊力斷金.” “San” means three. “Ren” means human being. “Tong” means same. “Xin” means heart. “Qi Li” means their strength. “Duan” means cut. “Jin” means gold bar. “San Ren Tong Xin, Qi Li Duan Jin” means that if three people have the same heart, their strength can cut through a gold bar.
In the modern world, think about any successful organization or any family. “Da He Xie” is the key for success. In a family, greatest harmony is the key for the family’s success in every aspect of life. In an organization or in a country, “Da He Xie,” greatest harmony, is the key for success. People understand teamwork and “Da He Xie” is the key principle for teamwork. A human being has many systems, organs, and cells. Every organ has millions or billions of cells. A human being has all types of relationships. A human being has a relationship with nature. “Da He Xie” is the sixth Source nature to reach kong.
“Da Cheng Sheng,” greatest flourishing. There is the ancient wisdom, “Tao Bu Yuan Ren, Ren Zi Yuan 道不遠人, 人自遠.” “Tao” means Source. “Bu” means not. “Yuan” means far. “Ren” means human being. “Zi” means yourself. “Tao Bu Yuan Ren, Ren Zi Yuan” means Tao, which is The Source, is not far from a human being, but a human being him or herself is far from Tao due to not applying Tao in life. Tao is like the Sun. Tao serves everything selflessly. Tao blesses everything equally. Tao bestows Source wisdom and blesses everyone to have Source success.
Why are some people successful and others not? One who is successful connects with Tao Source, appreciates Tao Source, and serves Tao Source in this lifetime and in previous lifetimes. To serve Tao Source is to serve others unconditionally, just like the Sun. Why is one not successful? It is because this one is not close to Tao Source and does not appreciate Tao Source enough or may not serve Tao Source and others unconditionally in this
lifetime and previous lifetimes. Do kind things; speak kind words. Tao Source will bless you more. Then success, “Da Cheng Sheng,” greatest flourishing will come to you. This is the seventh key to reach kong.
“Da Gan En,” greatest gratitude. Tao Source created human beings, Mother Earth, and countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes. “De 德,” which is virtue, nourishes a human being and countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes. “Shen Kou Yi 身 口 意” of Tao is “De.” “Shen” means action. “Kou” means speech. “Yi” means thought. Action, speech, and thought of Tao Source is virtue. Virtue is the spiritual nutrient to nourish every aspect of life. Love Tao Source. Appreciate Tao Source. This is step eight to reach kong.
“Da Fu Wu,” greatest service. Everyone serves. Parents serve their children. Employees serve their company. A human being serves society. “Da Fu Wu” is to serve humanity and all souls. Serve a little, receive a little blessing. Serve more, receive more blessings. Serve unconditionally, receive unlimited blessings. In my spiritual insight, the purpose of life is to serve. I have committed my life to this purpose. To serve is to make others happier and healthier. I am an unconditional universal servant. Serve. Serve. Serve. Every being needs to serve humanity and all souls more. This is step nine to reach kong.
“Da Yuan Man,” greatest enlightenment. A human being is made of shen qi jing, which is soul, heart, mind, and body. To reach enlightenment includes shen qi jing enlightenment. Enlighten the soul, heart, mind, and body. There are four major layers of saints including “Ren Xian 人仙,” Human Being saint; “Di Xian 地仙,” Mother Earth saint; “Tian Xian 天仙,” Heaven saint; and “Tao Xian 道仙,” Source saint.
Being a Human Saint means one has reached rejuvenation that transforms old age into the health and purity of the state of a baby.
A Mother Earth Saint is given the ability and honor to transform Mother Earth. Mother Earth is experiencing global warming. There are many natural disasters and imbalances between Heaven and Mother Earth. A Mother Earth saint transforms Mother Earth, including natural disasters.
A Heaven Saint is given the ability and honor to transform Heaven, including the weather in Heaven and many other aspects in Heaven.
A Tao Saint is given the ability and honor to transform Heaven and Mother Earth, as well as countless planets, stars, galaxies, and universes. Tao is The Source and Creator. Tao saints have the abilities of creation and
manifestation. This is the tenth key to reach kong. The Ten Da are the qualities needed to reach kong.
Let me summarize the key points of the timeless wisdom of China as I have recognized them.
In Buddhism, Taoism, Confucianism, and all spiritual realms, the highest wisdom and achievement—to become a Buddha, to reach Tao, and to achieve the level of a highest saint—this can all be summarized in one phrase: reach kong.
To reach kong, a spiritual being must remove all shen qi jing blockages including soul, heart, mind, and body blockages such as greed, anger, and lack of wisdom in activities, actions, behaviors, speech, and thought; all kinds of attachments and desires, including fame, power, and selfishness; and all kinds of spiritual, mental, emotional, and physical blockages.
To possess the Ten Da qualities is essential to reach kong. I summarize the wisdom I recognized in a single sentence: Kong is to become a Buddha, reach immortality, and become a highest
saint; as well as to purify, transform, and enlighten one’s shen qi jing to transform one’s shen qi jing to Source shen qi jing.
Kong kong kong is my spiritual realization. It is the path for every spiritual being who is searching to become a Buddha, to reach immortality, and to become the highest saint in all realms. To reach these objectives, we need to become a better servant.
I love my heart and soul I love all humanity Join hearts and souls together Love, peace, and harmony Love, peace, and harmony.

   APPENDIX: CONFIRMATORY EVIDENCE OF DUCK (AND ASSOCIATED GEESE) SYNCHRONICITIES
   by Gary Schwartz
   As I mentioned above, replicated evidence is essential for me to hold a belief and draw a conclusion, and I intentionally apply basic methods of science to the laboratory of my personal life. It is in the spirit of self-science that I have included this post-chapter appendix of uncanny additional duck synchronicities for the interested reader.
   Within an hour of the completion of the first draft of this chapter, a set of six duck synchronicities (i.e., a duck supersynchronicity) occurred in a two-day period. Then, a few days later, within an hour of the completion of the second draft of this chapter, an additional set of eleven duck (and geese) synchronicities occurred in a three-day period. The combined duck-goose supersynchronicity ends with what might be called the goose-wisdom lesson.
   These seventeen events are described as they unfolded so that the reader can better experience their complex, dynamic, sometimes silly, often mysterious, inspiring, and meaningful nature.
   Event #1: The first draft of this chapter was completed at three p.m. Arizona time on January 27, 2019. Coincidentally, Rhonda (my wife) and I had scheduled a “date” where we would have an early dinner at a restaurant near the Tucson Mall followed by our purchasing a gift at a store in the mall. On the way to the restaurant I mentioned to Rhonda that I had just finished the first draft of my chapter for the Laszlo book, but I did not tell her anything about the content.
   After dinner, we walked into the mall and Rhonda asked me, seemingly
   out of the blue, “Have you had any duck synchronicities lately?” I said, “What? Why do you ask?” Rhonda said, and I paraphrase for simplicity, “We are approaching a
   strange dispensing machine featuring ducks.” I could not believe my eyes—a six-foot-high, phone-booth-sized, brightly
   yellow-lit dispensing machine containing hundreds of little rubber ducks. We have seen maybe two or three machines of this type since the initial nineteen-duck-event supersynchronicity occurred almost a decade ago. For a dollar one could navigate a cranelike mechanism and try to retrieve a duck. Fortunately, Rhonda had a dollar bill (no pun intended), and I tried to manipulate the device. Though I tried to select a brightly colored little duck, what I retrieved was a humble plain yellow duck.
   To celebrate the propitious event and remember it in the future, I have named the little duck “Dr. Laszlo” in honor of the author of this volume.
   Event #2: Before going to sleep that night, Rhonda and I turned on the TV. On the guide was a TV show called Duck Tales (“Tales” is not a typo). Another beyond chance event? More humor from The
   I initially added Events #1 and #2 as a PS and PPS to the chapter and
   emailed this draft to Laszlo.
   Event #3: The next day I sent a follow-up email to Laszlo:
   Are you sitting down? Rhonda and I had breakfast this (Sunday) morning at a restaurant that recently moved to a
   new location. There are maybe thirty tables in the restaurant. The one table that was available, in the
   position I happened to be sitting, ended up looked directly at two statues of ducks! As far as I can ascertain, there are no other statues of ducks in this restaurant. How often do you spontaneously eat in a restaurant and find that your table and seat are
   facing multiple ducks (even one duck)? For me the answer is virtually never. I wonder if I should add a PS to honor The Source’s apparent awareness of my
   participation in your seminal volume?
   Event #4: The next morning (Monday), while Rhonda and I were traveling by car to Los Angeles (Rhonda was driving at the time), I received the following response to my email from Laszlo:
   Hello Gary—another one from this side: There is a poem that launched one of our invited writers on her path of spirituality and new
   science. The title of the poem is “The Duck,” and the author is Jane Goodall. It’s right in this book …
   At this point I began to wonder, was a new duck supersynchronicity in progress?
   Event #5: That night Rhonda and I had dinner with two producers working on a documentary featuring our ongoing SoulPhone research (www.soulphone.org). One them, M.H., eagerly told
   us that after finding a feather at my laboratory a year ago (they were doing preliminary filming at the time), which he perceived as meaningful at the time, he has since come upon approximately fifty feathers, which he has saved in an envelope. Since ducks have feathers, and he had spontaneously made a point to bring up the feathers, I asked him the following:
   “I know this will sound odd, but my intuition tells me I should ask you
   this question: Do you have any connection with ducks?” To my amazement, he replied, and I paraphrase slightly, “Not in the past
   few months. But every spring two ducks spend a few weeks enjoying our swimming pool.”
   Two (1 + 1=11) ducks in his swimming pool, every spring? I said to M.H., “You probably know many people who have private
   swimming pools. Do any of them have regular duck visitors?” He replied, “None.”
   Event #6: Inspired by M.H.’s unusual duck history, I decided to tell them about the Laszlo chapter and the emerging duck synchronicities. Since they were familiar with Canyon Ranch, I mentioned how I learned from Dr. K. that Dr. D. at that time lived on “Three Ducks Lane,” and later that day I literally bumped into Dr. D. and confirmed that he lived on the duck-named street.
   I then asked M.H. if he knew anyone who lived on a duck-named street.
   He did not. However, his colleague C.B. shared that he knew someone who lived on a duck-named street! What are the odds?
   Given M.H.’s association and appreciation of the two ducks’ regular visitations, and his apparent fondness for feathers, I confessed that during the initial nineteen-duck supersynchronicity that Dr. K.’s partner decided that I deserved the new title “Ductor.” One thing led to another, and we created a secret honorary “International Ductor Society.” Over a ten-year period we inducted (or as we playfully say, “induckted”) approximately a dozen honorary ductors (only a subset has MDs or PhDs).
   In my role as the honorary first ductor and founding president of the IDS, I invited M.H. to become Ductor H., and he gracefully accepted.
   Event #7: The following Saturday morning I finally had time to revise version 1.0 of this chapter. The following surprise email from a person I do not know (I will call him Mike) appeared in my inbox that morning. I am smiling as I insert it below:
   I am currently reading your book about the above and have to tell you about some things that happened this week.
   On Thursday I remarked to my wife that despite spending most of our time outdoors, I never see any geese in flight and haven’t done so for many years.
   I then had a call from an ex-police colleague to tell me of a small reunion next week and that “The Goose” would be there. Nickname for a guy named John Gosling.
   This morning my wife and I left the house to feed our horses and as we left a large flock of about thirty Canada geese flew over our heads! I was awestruck because it is many years since that has happened.
   As we left the horses, which is a mile further from home, a further small flock of geese flew overhead.
   As a former detective and dyed-in-the-wool skeptic, I now fully get it and share your sense of amazement!
   I await the next event in this sequence.
   Though geese and ducks, along with swans, belong to the same biological family, Anatidae, geese are not identical to ducks. I include this surprise email as Event #7 in light of the events that followed.
   Event # 8: After inserting Mike’s email above, Rhonda and I had an appointment in Scottsdale (about 100 miles from Tucson) to purchase a pre-owned roadster for my upcoming seventy-fifth birthday. As is our tradition, I typically drive the first half, and Rhonda drives the second half. Shortly after Rhonda became the driver, I checked my emails on my iPhone, and I could not believe what I was reading. The email was titled “Events 4&5” and read as follows:
   Hi Gary, Further to my email of earlier today. We had been out visiting relatives and got out of the car at home to hear and see another
   flock of geese overhead at 5 p.m. on the same day. It takes all of 5 seconds to get into the house from the car so we could so easily have
   missed that event. I then began reading your book again and immediately the chapter on ducks. The first line
   reads, “I don’t believe in Mother Goose”!
   How would you feel if you were in my shoes, driving to the Phoenix area, checking your email, and then you received such an email explicitly
   connecting duck and geese synchronicities? Was there a deeper meaning here?
   As you can probably imagine, I was inspired to read this email out loud to Rhonda. Then, literally within five minutes of sharing Mike’s email, Rhonda saw a license plate that read “MRDUCKS.” Fortunately, I had my iPhone handy and was able to capture this highly improbable and meaningful coincidence.
   FYI, equally amazing was that the MRDUCKS license plate sighting occurred at the Wild Horse Pass exit, and you will recall that Mike mentioned how his seeing the flock of geese occurred when he and his wife went to feed their horses. As it so happened, I was intentionally wearing a Native American horse pendant plus a complementary horse bracelet on the trip to Scottsdale, and our appointment indirectly involved a special horse pendant which we would be placing in our new roadster. Hmmm.…
   At this point it was obvious that there were too many novel and meaningful duck-related events that occurred following the writing of the first draft of this chapter to dismiss them as mere chance events or simply due to selective attention on our parts.
   Events #8 and #9: Though I did not say this to Rhonda, I asked myself (and implicitly the universe) if we would have a complementary goose event in Scottsdale.
   As it so happened, Rhonda and I were driving in different cars when we
   drove to a favorite restaurant in Old Town Scottsdale for lunch and then back to Tucson. There was a fair amount of traffic, and I was driving an unfamiliar car. Hence, I was not paying close attention to the surroundings. However, Rhonda was not so distracted, and she witnessed two ridiculously improbable events, the first on the trip to the restaurant, and the second on the trip returning home.
   As Rhonda confessed to me when we returned home, she literally witnessed two geese among a few ducks in some artificial lakes near the restaurant! Yes, live geese and ducks, at the same time, in Scottsdale, Arizona.
   What was especially odd was that on the trip to the restaurant, the two
   geese were literally walking on the grass near the road. Rhonda was able to photograph the geese and some ducks (from a distance) on the trip home as we coincidently drove past the lakes to the Route 101 (we could have taken various streets out of Scottsdale to return to Tucson).
   Though it is hard to see, there are approximately eleven ducks (no joke) in the photo (on the grass beyond the little lake, on the left) and two geese farther up toward the buildings (one standing, the other sitting).
   Eleven ducks? Two geese? Route 101? Uncanny coincidences, or a very clever and persistent Source? ☺.
   Events #10 and #11: The following morning (Monday), after adding the above duck (and geese) synchronicities, I sent the second draft to Laszlo with bcc’s to Mike, M.H., and C.B. for their feedback. I received the following email from Mike, which was super meaningful:
   Thanks so much, Gary. More geese last night and again this morning. Now taking photos and making notes as
   suggested in your book. This all makes me smile. Incidentally I mentioned visiting my grandson. Would you believe his name is Sam.
   A detective experiencing geese synchronicities, reading my duck synchronicity chapter, has a grandson named Sam? Sometimes the evidence is hard to believe.
   Events #12 to #15: That afternoon I was scheduled to give a brief update on our SoulPhone research to an international team of volunteers working with Dr. Mark Pitstick in the SoulPhone Foundation. After my update, I felt inspired to ask the twelve people on the conference call whether any of them had any
   unusual or meaning experiences with ducks and/or geese lately. Two people shared propitious and meaningful duck and/or geese events. I include portions of their follow-up emails below.
   Event #12 concerns the recent photographing of ducks and geese with a
   new camera lens and associated synchronous events:
   When you asked if anyone in our online meeting had experienced anything having to do with ducks or geese in the last two weeks, I immediately raised my hand. Recently I bought myself a big new telephoto lens. I had been shooting with an 18–200mm zoom lens, but I wanted something more powerful, so I bought a 200–500mm zoom lens along with a new Nikon D500. These monster lenses and new camera require practice to learn how to use with any skill, and I am not there yet.
   With improving weather over the last couple of weeks I have taken several trips to nearby national wildlife refuges including a couple that were new to me, Snag Boat Bend near Corvallis, Oregon, and Talking Water Gardens in Albany, Oregon. I was looking for flying geese and other waterfowl along with my other favorite thing to photograph: interesting water reflections.
   On January 25, 2019, I was out at Ankeny National Wildlife Refuge south of my house in Salem looking for geese. I found a flock sitting in a field but nothing was happening worth photographing. While waiting for something to happen, I was most surprised when who should pull up in their car but my sister and brother-in-law. They live about an hour away from me, and we had no idea the other was in the area!
   On January 31, 2019, I went to Talking Water Gardens, where waterfowl are wintering until sometime in March. Swimming ducks are a lot easier to photograph than flying geese, and besides, there were very few geese at this venue. Also interesting was the plant life that turned the water into what made it look like the ducks were swimming in a green carpet. I took quite a few shots and included a couple here.
   Something else happened that day that I found interesting in light of your duck synchronicities. A year or so ago with my much shorter lens I was photographing some flying geese from fairly far away. I noticed that for a split second, it almost looked as if the geese had formed a constellation-like drawing of a hummingbird. Today I was scrolling through my Ankeny geese photos and one jumped out at me. The top half of the photo (geese form duck) to me resembled a duck. I don’t know if anyone else would see a duck, but that was the first thought I had when I scrolled through my photos.
   Events #13 and #14 involves a reading with a medium connected to a photograph with a duck, followed by an atypical geese sighting:
   Two weeks ago, I had a reading with a medium. In the reading she said that Aymen said, “Mom, what is with the goofy picture you have hanging in your office of me?” The picture I have hanging is attached—a large framed photo of Aymen holding a baby duck. I have been looking at the picture for the last two weeks deciding if I need to change the picture because
   Aymen called it goofy…:) Also, I walk every day to be in nature. A few days ago, I was walking and saw a V-shaped
   formation of geese. I counted the number of them—there were ten. I thought to myself, Why are geese in Colorado…? I thought they fly south for the winter.
   I love the synchronicity—thanks for asking.
   Event #15: That night, I turned on the TV and discovered that an episode of the series How the Universe Works was devoted to the possible evidence for black holes. When I clicked on the show in progress, within two minutes a male astrophysicist appeared and literally used the “If it walks like a duck” expression! I could not believe my ears. Thanks to instant reply, plus iPhone video recording, I was able to make a recording of his exact words:
   Whatever it is that we are seeing, if it smells like a black hole, walks like a black hole, and quacks like a black hole, it’s a black hole.
   This astrophysicist is more emphatic than I am, since I would say, “it’s probably a black hole.” However, the essence of the message is the same.
   Event #16: I immediately switched to another TV show that was just starting, called America’s Got Talent: The Champions. One of the first contestants was an eighteen-year-old opera singer who first appeared on the show when she was ten years old. Videos shown concerning her early life and how it changed after her sensational performances eight
   years earlier. The videos including her with two geese followed by two ducks! As you can imagine, we replayed the videos and took photos of her with the geese and ducks.
   The odds of the astrophysicist using the duck metaphor, followed by the
   young singer shown with geese and ducks, are extraordinarily improbable.
   Event #17: It seems like the universe is reinforcing the primary take-home messages of this chapter:
   A Source exists. This ultra-intelligent (and playful) Source connects with us and we with it. We can consciously interact with it. It can provide us with real-life evidence that it is playing a meaningful role in everything, including our lives.
   However, is there more to the message? Does the addition of the geese provide additional wisdom?
   The following email from Mike suggests that it does. He wrote:
   I told my daughter about the geese on Saturday and she went to work today to find this on the wall!
   She was off work yesterday and had not spoken to anyone about it.
   I asked for more details and Mike responded:
   My daughter supervises a team of social workers on child protection. This is the only time anything related to animals has ever been posted on the wall. One of her staff posted it to illustrate the benefits of team work.
   This is her text when I queried whether she had been discussing it in the office: Did they know the story xxx no I was off yesterday xx “just a random story about teamwork” x
   The odds of the sequence of events must surely be astronomical now! The story posted on the wall was simply called “The Goose Story” and
   the author was listed as “Author Unknown.” Was this posting “just a random story about teamwork”? Or was it something more?
   In searching the internet, I learned that a story called “Lessons from the Geese” was purportedly written in 1972 by Dr. Robert McNeish of Baltimore, Maryland. Apparently, McNeish, a science teacher, had for many years been intrigued by his observation of geese. A PowerPoint presentation based on McNeish’s lay sermon is available on the internet.
   As interpreted by Mike Ford in an essay he wrote in 2000 for his church, the five lessons of the geese are:
   Cooperation Unity Leadership Encouragement Family
   Is it possible that these wisdom lessons/virtues relate to the higher purpose of Reconnecting to The Source? Are we being invited to reflect on this possible reminder from The Source?
   The journey continues …
   ACKNOWLEDGMENTS
   IT IS not only a moral duty, but a distinct personal pleasure to acknowledge the remarkable contribution to this book of many outstanding individuals, both friends and colleagues. They wrote in frank and clear terms of experiences of such nonordinary dimension that we would normally hesitate to speak of them. The description of their experiences not only makes for fascinating reading; it confirms the thesis of this book, that discovering our own fundamental nature holds the key to ensuring our individual well-being as well as the future of our species.
   I wish I could thank each contributor individually, but for lack of time and space, I hope they will accept this collective expression of my heartfelt thanks. The contributors whom I wish to thank from the bottom of my heart are (in alphabetical order):
   LAWRENCE BLOOM
   DARLA BOONE
   NICOLYA CHRISTI
   FEDERICO FAGGIN
   ADRIENNE FELLER
   GUIDO FERRARI
   JANE GOODALL
   JEAN HOUSTON
   BARBARA MARX HUBBARD
   CHRISTOPHER LASZLO
   LYNNE MCTAGGART
   JAMES O’DEA
   FRÉDÉRIQUE PICHARD
   PIERRE PRADERVAND
   MASAMI SAIONJI
   GARY SCHWARTZ
   Among these brilliant contributors, Nicolya Christi and Maria Sági have again proved to be my soulmates, offering invaluable inspiration and guidance.
   Beyond those who shared their own spiritual experiences, I wish to acknowledge the contribution of two of my esteemed friends and collaborators, Frederick Chavalit Tsao, and Zhi-Gang Sha. Last but not least, I am grateful to four of my close collaborators: Kingsley Dennis, Alexander Laszlo, Shamik Desai, and Mark Gober. Their insights and wisdom document and elaborate the ideas I hope to have put forward in this book.
   Again, as many times before, I am indebted to my longtime friend Deepak Chopra for the wise words of his foreword. He could situate the objective and aspiration of this volume in the context of our everyday occupations and preoccupations, encapsulating in a few pages what the book intends to communicate about spiritual experience, and its ability to reveal the nature and the importance of our connection to The Source.
   In regard to the practical aspect of the project to bring this work to the public, I am delighted to acknowledge the vital role of my literary agent and friend of many years Bill Gladstone, and Gayle, his wonderful partner and now spouse. They have remained expert guides and precious supporters over the years. A particularly deep vote of thanks goes to my editor Joel Fotinos, assisted by Gwen Hawkes and the editorial and PR team at St. Martin’s Press. Joel’s constant and consistent encouragement and unwavering belief, together with his team’s outstanding expertise, have been major factors in presenting Reconnecting to The Source to an interested, and we hope extensive, public.
   Last but not least I wish to thank my wife, Carita, for again providing a rock-solid foundation for my life during the seemingly never-ending writes and rewrites of the manuscript. Without her loving presence I could not have written this book, because I could not have existed in the peace and well-being I needed to formulate the thoughts I wanted to express.
   I am fortunate indeed to have friends and colleagues and family of this caliber. They have not only affirmed, but have brilliantly illustrated the bold thesis that deep down we are wholeness- and love-oriented holotropic beings, capable of co-existing and co-flourishing with each other and with all the
   inhabitants of this precious planet.
   ERVIN LASZLO September 2019

   Praise for Reconnecting to The Source
   “Based on the themes of oneness and unity in modern science and psychology, and leavened by deep spiritual lessons that are part of the human legacy, a way opens toward a bright, positive future for humanity. It is difficult to imagine a more important message for our time.” —LARRY DOSSEY, MD, AUTHOR OF ONE MIND: HOW OUR INDIVIDUAL MIND IS PART
   OF A GREATER CONSCIOUSNESS AND WHY IT MATTERS
   “In Reconnecting to The Source, Ervin Laszlo gives us a compelling way forward, indeed, the only way forward through the confusion and chaos of the mounting crises of the twenty-first century. Read this gem of a book carefully and share it with your friends. They will thank you and so will your grandchildren.”
   —CHRISTOPHER BACHE, PHD, AUTHOR OF LSD AND THE MIND OF THE UNIVERSE
   AND DARK NIGHT, EARLY DAWN
   “Laszlo has written an extremely important book that allows us to understand that through spiritual experience we perceive ourselves as part of the world, and through this perception we avoid disaster and can build a better world.” —PIER MARIO BIAVA, MD, AUTHOR OF INFORMATION MEDICINE: REVOLUTIONARY
   CELL-REPROGRAMMING DISCOVERY THAT REVERSES CANCER AND DEGENERATIVE
   “An elucidating read … Ervin Laszlo’s stunning compilation helps us navigate a necessary new Hero’s Journey; one of Individuality as part of Immensity—and a true Source of one-der.”
   —ALISON GOLDWYN, FOUNDER AND CREATIVE DIRECTOR OF SYNCHRONISTORY
   (TELEVISION EVENT)

Dr. Laszlo makes the compelling case that in today’s
   troubled world, reconnecting to The Source is not just an option but an urgent necessity. This book is a wake-up call for humanity.”
   —MARK GOBER, AUTHOR OF AN END TO UPSIDE DOWN THINKING
   “This is a book of great wonderment and hope, lodged heartily within the annals of a maverick revolution in physics and evolutionary biology. A book at once both page-turner, and abiding source of deep reflection. A monumental achievement.”
   —MICHAEL TOBIAS, PRESIDENT OF DANCING STAR FOUNDATION
   “Reconnecting to The Source is a profound look at the future we will create when we listen to our better angels. Ervin Laszlo is one of those angels.”

   “Reconnecting to The Source is a vital, timely, and eminently practical guide to support our healing and experiential re-membering of the wholeness of who we really are.”
   —DR. JUDE CURRIVAN, PHD, COSMOLOGIST, HEALER, AND AUTHOR OF THE

   “Ervin Laszlo has once again expanded our view of life on Earth and beyond. He offers both age-old and new ways to know ourselves as intrinsically connected, and to gain a deeper understanding of the effects of our actions on the whole.”
   —ANNE-MARIE VOORHOEVE, STRATEGY DIRECTOR FOR THE CLUB OF BUDAPEST
   (INTERNATIONAL NETWORK)

   takes us there. It is well worth your attention.” —STEPHAN A. SCHWARTZ, AUTHOR OF THE EIGHT LAWS OF CHANGE
   “On these inspiring pages philosopher and sage Ervin Laszlo, twice nominated for the Nobel Prize, reawakens in us the ancient Hermetic truth, ‘as above, so below.’” —ALLAN COMBS, CALIFORNIA INSTITUTE FOR INTEGRAL STUDIES, AUTHOR OF THE

   ABOUT THE AUTHOR
   Ervin Laszlo is President of the Laszlo Institute of New Paradigm Research, Founder and President of The Club of Budapest, Fellow of the World Academy of Art and Science, Member of the Hungarian Academy of Science and of the International Academy of Philosophy of Science, Senator of the International Medici Academy, and Editor of the periodical World Futures: The Journal of New Paradigm Research. He is the recipient of the Goi Peace Prize (2002), the International Mandir of Peace Prize (2005), the Conacreis Holistic Culture Prize (2009), the Ethics Prize of Milano (2014), and the Luxembourg World Peace Prize (2017). He was nominated for the Nobel Peace Prize in 2006 and 2007. He holds Honorary Ph. D.’s from the United States, Canada, Finland, and Hungary. Ervin Laszlo is the author of more than sixty books translated into twenty-six languages, including Information Medicine: A New Paradigm for the Health Sciences co-authored with Pier Mario Biava (Inner Traditions, Rochester VT. 2019), and The Tuscany Dialogues: The Earth, Our Future, and the Scope of Human Consciousness, with Michael Tobias (Select Books/Eco-Prime, New York, 2019).
